(function(){
    try{
        var key = "ppusa_tz";
        var tz = (Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone||"").trim();
        if(!tz) return;
        var prev = null;
        try { prev = localStorage.getItem(key); } catch(e) {}
        if (prev === tz) return;
        fetch("/api/ajax/tz", {
            method:"POST",
            headers:{
                "Content-Type":"application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8",
                "X-Requested-With":"XMLHttpRequest"
            },
            body:"tz="+encodeURIComponent(tz)
        }).then(function(res){

            }
        }).catch(function(){});
    }catch(e){}
})();
